--- Returngo/app_classes.py ---

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Dict

@dataclass
class UserSettings:
    theme: str = "dark"  # dark, light, auto
    favorites: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    export_presets: Dict[str, Dict] = field(default_factory=dict)
    performance_metrics: bool = True
    keyboard_shortcuts: bool = True

@dataclass
class PerformanceMetrics:
    api_call_times: List[float] = field(default_factory=list)
    cache_hit_rate: float = 0.0
    last_sync_duration: float = 0.0
    avg_response_time: float = 0.0

class Theme(Enum):
    DARK = "dark"
    LIGHT = "light"
    AUTO = "auto"


--- Returngo/bootstrap.ps1 ---

param(
    [Parameter(Position=0)]
    [ValidatePattern('^(stable|latest|\d+\.\d+\.\d+(-[^\s]+)?)$')]
    [string]$Target = "latest"
)

Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ProgressPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'

# Check for 32-bit Windows
if (-not [Environment]::Is64BitProcess) {
    Write-Error "Claude Code does not support 32-bit Windows. Please use a 64-bit version of Windows."
    exit 1
}

$GCS_BUCKET = "https://storage.googleapis.com/claude-code-dist-86c565f3-f756-42ad-8dfa-d59b1c096819/claude-code-releases"
$DOWNLOAD_DIR = "$env:USERPROFILE\.claude\downloads"

# Always use x64 for Windows (ARM64 Windows can run x64 through emulation)
$platform = "win32-x64"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $DOWNLOAD_DIR | Out-Null

# Always download latest version (which has the most up-to-date installer)
try {
    $version = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$GCS_BUCKET/latest" -ErrorAction Stop
}
catch {
    Write-Error "Failed to get latest version: $_"
    exit 1
}

try {
    $manifest = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$GCS_BUCKET/$version/manifest.json" -ErrorAction Stop
    $checksum = $manifest.platforms.$platform.checksum

    if (-not $checksum) {
        Write-Error "Platform $platform not found in manifest"
        exit 1
    }
}
catch {
    Write-Error "Failed to get manifest: $_"
    exit 1
}

# Download and verify
$binaryPath = "$DOWNLOAD_DIR\claude-$version-$platform.exe"
try {
    Invoke-WebRequest -Uri "$GCS_BUCKET/$version/$platform/claude.exe" -OutFile $binaryPath -ErrorAction Stop
}
catch {
    Write-Error "Failed to download binary: $_"
    if (Test-Path $binaryPath) {
        Remove-Item -Force $binaryPath
    }
    exit 1
}

# Calculate checksum
$actualChecksum = (Get-FileHash -Path $binaryPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLower()

if ($actualChecksum -ne $checksum) {
    Write-Error "Checksum verification failed"
    Remove-Item -Force $binaryPath
    exit 1
}

# Run claude install to set up launcher and shell integration
Write-Output "Setting up Claude Code..."
try {
    if ($Target) {
        & $binaryPath install $Target
    }
    else {
        & $binaryPath install
    }
}
finally {
    try {
        # Clean up downloaded file
        # Wait a moment for any file handles to be released
        Start-Sleep -Seconds 1
        Remove-Item -Force $binaryPath
    }
    catch {
        Write-Warning "Could not remove temporary file: $binaryPath"
    }
}

Write-Output ""
Write-Output "$([char]0x2705) Installation complete!"
Write-Output ""

<body class="bg-slate-100 text-slate-900">
    <div id="root"></div>

    <script type="text/babel">
        const { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } = React;

        const PERIOD_START = "2026-01-01";
        const PERIOD_END = "2026-05-01";
        const MONTHS = ["2026-01", "2026-02", "2026-03", "2026-04", "2026-05"];
        const MONTH_LABELS = { "2026-01": "Jan", "2026-02": "Feb", "2026-03": "Mar", "2026-04": "Apr", "2026-05": "May 1" };
        const ECOMPLETE = "#002244";
        const STORES = [
            { prefix: "D", id: "diesel", name: "Diesel", color: "#E4002B" },
            { prefix: "H", id: "hurley", name: "Hurley", color: "#00AEEF" },
            { prefix: "J", id: "jeep", name: "Jeep Apparel", color: "#F8BD21" },
            { prefix: "R", id: "reebok", name: "Reebok", color: "#9E2326" },
            { prefix: "S", id: "superdry", name: "Superdry", color: "#FF5E00" }
        ];
        const STORE_BY_PREFIX = Object.fromEntries(STORES.map(s => [s.prefix, s]));

        const currency = (value, decimals = 0) => `R${(Number(value) || 0).toLocaleString("en-ZA", { minimumFractionDigits: decimals, maximumFractionDigits: decimals })}`;
        const pct = (value, decimals = 1) => Number.isFinite(value) ? `${value.toFixed(decimals)}%` : "n/a";
        const number = value => (Number(value) || 0).toLocaleString("en-ZA");
        const clean = value => value === null || value === undefined ? "" : String(value).trim();
        const safeLower = value => clean(value).toLowerCase();

        function dateKey(value) {
            if (value instanceof Date && !isNaN(value)) return value.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
            if (typeof value === "number") {
                const parsed = XLSX.SSF.parse_date_code(value);
                if (!parsed) return "";
                return `${parsed.y}-${String(parsed.m).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(parsed.d).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            }
            const text = clean(value);
            if (!text) return "";
            const iso = text.match(/(\d{4})[-/](\d{1,2})[-/](\d{1,2})/);
            if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${String(iso[2]).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(iso[3]).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            const d = new Date(text);
            if (!isNaN(d)) return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            return "";
        }

        function inPeriod(key) {
            return key >= PERIOD_START && key <= PERIOD_END;
        }

        function parseMoney(value) {
            if (typeof value === "number") return value;
            const text = clean(value).replace(/[^\d.-]/g, "");
            const parsed = Number(text);
            return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
        }

        function parseNumber(value, fallback = 0) {
            const parsed = Number(clean(value).replace(/,/g, ""));
            return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : fallback;
        }

        function brandFromOrder(orderId) {
            const prefix = clean(orderId).charAt(0).toUpperCase();
            return STORE_BY_PREFIX[prefix] || { prefix, id: "unknown", name: "Unknown", color: "#64748b" };
        }

        function resolutionGroup(value) {
            const text = safeLower(value);
            if (text.includes("exchange")) return "Exchange";
            if (text.includes("gift")) return "Gift Card";
            if (text.includes("credit") || text.includes("store credit") || text.includes("online account")) return "Store Credit";
            if (text.includes("cancel")) return "Cancel Order";
            if (text.includes("refund") || text.includes("payment")) return "Refund";
            return clean(value) || "Unknown";
        }

        function normalizeReturnRow(row, index) {
            const orderId = clean(row["Order ID"] || row["Order"] || row["Name"]);
            const rmaId = clean(row["RMA#"] || row["RMA"] || row["RMA ID"]) || `RMA-ROW-${index + 1}`;
            const key = dateKey(row["Date"] || row["Created Date"] || row["Date Return Requested"]);
            if (!orderId || !key || !inPeriod(key)) return null;
            const store = brandFromOrder(orderId);
            const qty = Math.max(1, parseNumber(row["Amount"], 1));
            const amountPaid = parseMoney(row["Amount Paid"]);
            const valueFallback = parseMoney(row["Balance"]) || parseMoney(row["Cash Refund"]) || parseMoney(row["Credit Refund"]) || parseMoney(row["Gift-Card Refund"]);
            const itemValue = amountPaid || valueFallback;
            const sku = clean(row["SKU"]);
            const productId = clean(row["Product ID"]);
            const variantId = clean(row["Variant ID"]);
            const barcode = clean(row["Barcode"]);
            const productKey = sku || productId || variantId || barcode || "Unknown product";
            return {
                rowId: `${rmaId}-${index}`,
                orderId,
                rmaId,
                dateKey: key,
                monthKey: key.slice(0, 7),
                storeId: store.id,
                storeName: store.name,
                storeColor: store.color,
                qty,
                sku,
                barcode,
                productId,
                variantId,
                productKey,
                returnType: clean(row["Return Type"]) || "Unknown",
                status: clean(row["Status"]) || "Unknown",
                resolution: clean(row["Resolutions"]) || clean(row["Resolution"]) || "Unknown",
                resolutionGroup: resolutionGroup(row["Resolutions"] || row["Resolution"]),
                reason: clean(row["Return Reason"]) || "Unknown",
                answer: clean(row["Answer"]) || "-",
                value: itemValue * qty,
                amountPaid: itemValue,
                orderCreatedKey: dateKey(row["Order create date"]),
                fulfilledKey: dateKey(row["Item Fulfillment Date"]),
                refundedKey: dateKey(row["Refunded/credited at"]),
                shippedBackKey: dateKey(row["Shipped back at"]),
                deliveredKey: dateKey(row["Delivered to store at"])
            };
        }

        function normalizeShopifyRows(rows) {
            const byOrder = new Map();
            rows.forEach(row => {
                const orderName = clean(row["Name"] || row["Order Name"] || row["Order"]);
                const key = dateKey(row["Created At"] || row["Created at"] || row["Date"]);
                if (!orderName || !key || !inPeriod(key)) return;
                const existing = byOrder.get(orderName);
                const price = parseMoney(row["Price: Total"] || row["Total"] || row["Total Price"]);
                if (!existing) {
                    const store = brandFromOrder(orderName);
                    byOrder.set(orderName, {
                        orderName,
                        dateKey: key,
                        monthKey: key.slice(0, 7),
                        storeId: store.id,
                        storeName: store.name,
                        storeColor: store.color,
                        value: price
                    });
                } else if (price > existing.value) {
                    existing.value = price;
                }
            });
            return [...byOrder.values()];
        }

        async function readTabularFile(file) {
            const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
            const workbook = XLSX.read(buffer, { type: "array", cellDates: true });
            const firstSheet = workbook.SheetNames[0];
            const sheet = workbook.Sheets[firstSheet];
            return XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, { defval: "", raw: false });
        }

        function uniqueCount(rows, key) {
            return new Set(rows.map(row => row[key]).filter(Boolean)).size;
        }

        function groupRows(rows, keyFn) {
            const map = new Map();
            rows.forEach(row => {
                const key = keyFn(row);
                if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, []);
                map.get(key).push(row);
            });
            return map;
        }

        function topValue(mapLike, fallback = "n/a") {
            const entries = Object.entries(mapLike || {}).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
            return entries[0]?.[0] || fallback;
        }

        function avgDays(startKey, endKey) {
            if (!startKey || !endKey) return null;
            const start = new Date(`${startKey}T00:00:00`);
            const end = new Date(`${endKey}T00:00:00`);
            if (isNaN(start) || isNaN(end) || end < start) return null;
            return Math.round((end - start) / 86400000);
        }

        function buildAnalytics(returnRows, shopifyRows, activeStore, search) {
            const searchText = safeLower(search);
            const scopedReturns = returnRows.filter(row => {
                const storeMatch = activeStore === "all" || row.storeId === activeStore;
                const searchMatch = !searchText || [row.orderId, row.rmaId, row.sku, row.productId, row.variantId, row.reason, row.answer, row.resolution, row.status]
                    .some(value => safeLower(value).includes(searchText));
                return storeMatch && searchMatch;
            });
            const scopedOrders = shopifyRows.filter(row => activeStore === "all" || row.storeId === activeStore);

            const returnedOrders = uniqueCount(scopedReturns, "orderId");
            const rmas = uniqueCount(scopedReturns, "rmaId");
            const items = scopedReturns.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0);
            const value = scopedReturns.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0);
            const shopifyOrderCount = scopedOrders.length;
            const shopifyValue = scopedOrders.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0);
            const returnRate = shopifyOrderCount ? returnedOrders / shopifyOrderCount * 100 : null;
            const valueRate = shopifyValue ? value / shopifyValue * 100 : null;

            const monthRows = MONTHS.map(month => {
                const returns = scopedReturns.filter(row => row.monthKey === month);
                const orders = scopedOrders.filter(row => row.monthKey === month);
                const returnedOrdersMonth = uniqueCount(returns, "orderId");
                return {
                    month,
                    label: MONTH_LABELS[month],
                    rmas: uniqueCount(returns, "rmaId"),
                    items: returns.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0),
                    returnedOrders: returnedOrdersMonth,
                    orders: orders.length,
                    rate: orders.length ? returnedOrdersMonth / orders.length * 100 : null
                };
            });

            const reasonCounts = {};
            const resolutionCounts = {};
            const statusCounts = {};
            const brandReasonMatrix = {};
            scopedReturns.forEach(row => {
                reasonCounts[row.reason] = (reasonCounts[row.reason] || 0) + row.qty;
                resolutionCounts[row.resolutionGroup] = (resolutionCounts[row.resolutionGroup] || 0) + row.qty;
                statusCounts[row.status] = (statusCounts[row.status] || 0) + row.qty;
                if (!brandReasonMatrix[row.storeName]) brandReasonMatrix[row.storeName] = {};
                brandReasonMatrix[row.storeName][row.reason] = (brandReasonMatrix[row.storeName][row.reason] || 0) + row.qty;
            });

            const brandsToShow = activeStore === "all" ? STORES : STORES.filter(store => store.id === activeStore);
            const brandRows = brandsToShow.map(store => {
                const returns = scopedReturns.filter(row => row.storeId === store.id);
                const orders = shopifyRows.filter(row => row.storeId === store.id);
                const reason = {};
                const resolution = {};
                returns.forEach(row => {
                    reason[row.reason] = (reason[row.reason] || 0) + row.qty;
                    resolution[row.resolutionGroup] = (resolution[row.resolutionGroup] || 0) + row.qty;
                });
                const returned = uniqueCount(returns, "orderId");
                const orderValue = orders.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0);
                const returnValue = returns.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0);
                return {
                    ...store,
                    rmas: uniqueCount(returns, "rmaId"),
                    returnedOrders: returned,
                    orderVolume: orders.length,
                    returnRate: orders.length ? returned / orders.length * 100 : null,
                    items: returns.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0),
                    value: returnValue,
                    valueRate: orderValue ? returnValue / orderValue * 100 : null,
                    topReason: topValue(reason),
                    topResolution: topValue(resolution)
                };
            }).sort((a, b) => (b.returnRate || 0) - (a.returnRate || 0));

            const reasonRows = [...groupRows(scopedReturns, row => row.reason)].map(([reason, rows]) => {
                const brandSplit = {};
                const answers = {};
                MONTHS.forEach(month => brandSplit[month] = 0);
                rows.forEach(row => {
                    brandSplit[row.storeName] = (brandSplit[row.storeName] || 0) + row.qty;
                    if (row.answer && row.answer !== "-") answers[row.answer] = (answers[row.answer] || 0) + row.qty;
                });
                return {
                    reason,
                    items: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0),
                    rmas: uniqueCount(rows, "rmaId"),
                    value: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0),
                    share: items ? rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0) / items * 100 : 0,
                    topAnswer: topValue(answers, "-"),
                    brandSplit
                };
            }).sort((a, b) => b.items - a.items);

            const resolutionRows = [...groupRows(scopedReturns, row => row.resolutionGroup)].map(([resolution, rows]) => ({
                resolution,
                items: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0),
                rmas: uniqueCount(rows, "rmaId"),
                value: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0),
                share: items ? rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0) / items * 100 : 0,
                brands: Object.fromEntries(STORES.map(store => [store.name, rows.filter(row => row.storeId === store.id).reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0)]))
            })).sort((a, b) => b.items - a.items);

            const productRows = [...groupRows(scopedReturns, row => row.productKey)].map(([productKey, rows]) => {
                const reasons = {};
                const resolutions = {};
                rows.forEach(row => {
                    reasons[row.reason] = (reasons[row.reason] || 0) + row.qty;
                    resolutions[row.resolutionGroup] = (resolutions[row.resolutionGroup] || 0) + row.qty;
                });
                return {
                    productKey,
                    sku: rows.find(row => row.sku)?.sku || "-",
                    barcode: rows.find(row => row.barcode)?.barcode || "-",
                    productId: rows.find(row => row.productId)?.productId || "-",
                    variantId: rows.find(row => row.variantId)?.variantId || "-",
                    items: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0),
                    rmas: uniqueCount(rows, "rmaId"),
                    value: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0),
                    topReason: topValue(reasons),
                    topResolution: topValue(resolutions)
                };
            }).sort((a, b) => b.items - a.items);

            const statusRows = [...groupRows(scopedReturns, row => row.status)].map(([status, rows]) => ({
                status,
                items: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0),
                rmas: uniqueCount(rows, "rmaId"),
                share: items ? rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.qty, 0) / items * 100 : 0
            })).sort((a, b) => b.items - a.items);

            const lifecycleRows = scopedReturns.map(row => ({
                receivedDays: avgDays(row.shippedBackKey, row.deliveredKey),
                refundDays: avgDays(row.dateKey, row.refundedKey),
                fulfillmentToReturnDays: avgDays(row.fulfilledKey || row.orderCreatedKey, row.dateKey)
            }));
            const average = key => {
                const vals = lifecycleRows.map(row => row[key]).filter(value => value !== null);
                return vals.length ? vals.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / vals.length : null;
            };

            const topReason = reasonRows[0]?.reason || "n/a";
            const topResolution = resolutionRows[0]?.resolution || "n/a";
            const highestRiskBrand = brandRows.find(row => row.orderVolume > 0 && row.returnRate !== null) || brandRows[0];
            const refundShare = resolutionRows.find(row => row.resolution === "Refund")?.share || 0;
            const lastFullMonth = monthRows.find(row => row.month === "2026-04");
            const priorMonth = monthRows.find(row => row.month === "2026-03");
            const momChange = lastFullMonth && priorMonth && priorMonth.items ? (lastFullMonth.items - priorMonth.items) / priorMonth.items * 100 : null;
            const skuConcentration = productRows[0] && items ? productRows[0].items / items * 100 : 0;
            const commentary = [
                shopifyOrderCount ? `${highestRiskBrand?.name || "The leading brand"} shows the highest order-based return rate at ${pct(highestRiskBrand?.returnRate || 0)}.` : "Upload Shopify orders to unlock return-rate benchmarking against order volume.",
                `${topReason} is the dominant return reason, representing ${pct(reasonRows[0]?.share || 0)} of returned items.`,
                `${topResolution} is the leading customer resolution preference; refunds account for ${pct(refundShare)} of returned items.`,
                momChange === null ? "Month-over-month commentary requires March and April return volume." : `April return item volume ${momChange >= 0 ? "increased" : "decreased"} by ${pct(Math.abs(momChange))} versus March.`,
                productRows[0] ? `The top SKU/Product ID contributes ${pct(skuConcentration)} of returned items, so SKU-level review is ${skuConcentration >= 5 ? "material" : "currently dispersed"}.` : "SKU/Product concentration will appear after ReturnGO upload."
            ];

            return {
                scopedReturns,
                scopedOrders,
                kpis: { rmas, returnedOrders, items, value, shopifyOrderCount, shopifyValue, returnRate, valueRate },
                monthRows,
                reasonCounts,
                resolutionCounts,
                statusCounts,
                brandRows,
                reasonRows,
                resolutionRows,
                productRows,
                statusRows,
                brandReasonMatrix,
                lifecycle: {
                    avgReceivedDays: average("receivedDays"),
                    avgRefundDays: average("refundDays"),
                    avgFulfillmentToReturnDays: average("fulfillmentToReturnDays")
                },
                commentary
            };
        }

        function ChartPanel({ title, subtitle, config, compact = false }) {
            const canvasRef = useRef(null);
            const chartRef = useRef(null);
            useEffect(() => {
                if (!canvasRef.current || !window.Chart) return;
                if (chartRef.current) chartRef.current.destroy();
                chartRef.current = new Chart(canvasRef.current, config);
                return () => chartRef.current && chartRef.current.destroy();
            }, [JSON.stringify(config)]);
            return (
                <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm p-5">
                    <div className="mb-4">
                        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900">{title}</h3>
                        {subtitle && <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{subtitle}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div className={compact ? "compact-chart" : "chart-box"}>
                        <canvas ref={canvasRef}></canvas>
                    </div>
                </section>
            );
        }

        function UploadCard({ title, required, file, onChange, icon, note }) {
            return (
                <label className="block bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm p-5 hover:border-slate-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                    <input type="file" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv" className="hidden" onChange={onChange} />
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                        <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-white" style={{ backgroundColor: required ? ECOMPLETE : "#64748b" }}>
                            <i className={`fa-solid ${icon}`}></i>
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <h3 className="font-bold">{title}</h3>
                                <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${required ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700" : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"}`}>{required ? "Required" : "Optional"}</span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">{file ? file.name : note}</p>
                        </div>
                        <i className="fa-solid fa-upload text-slate-300 mt-2"></i>
                    </div>
                </label>
            );
        }

        function KpiCard({ label, value, sub, icon, tone = "slate" }) {
            const tones = {
                slate: "bg-slate-900 text-white",
                blue: "bg-blue-700 text-white",
                green: "bg-emerald-700 text-white",
                amber: "bg-amber-500 text-slate-950",
                white: "bg-white text-slate-900 border border-slate-200"
            };
            return (
                <div className={`metric-card rounded-lg shadow-sm p-5 relative overflow-hidden ${tones[tone]}`}>
                    <i className={`fa-solid ${icon} absolute right-5 top-5 opacity-20 text-4xl`}></i>
                    <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide opacity-70 font-bold">{label}</p>
                    <p className="text-3xl font-black mt-3">{value}</p>
                    <p className="text-sm mt-2 opacity-75">{sub}</p>
                </div>
            );
        }

        function DataTable({ title, rows, columns, maxHeight = "max-h-[520px]", hideHeader = false }) {
            return (
                <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
                    {!hideHeader && (
                        <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900">{title}</h3>
                            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">{number(rows.length)} rows</span>
                        </div>
                    )}
                    <div className={`overflow-auto custom-scroll ${maxHeight}`}>
                        <table className="min-w-full text-sm">
                            <thead className="sticky top-0 bg-white text-slate-500 uppercase text-[10px] tracking-wide border-b border-slate-200 z-10">
                                <tr>{columns.map(col => <th key={col.key} className={`px-4 py-3 text-left whitespace-nowrap ${col.align === "right" ? "text-right" : ""}`}>{col.label}</th>)}</tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                                {rows.map((row, idx) => (
                                    <tr key={idx} className="hover:bg-slate-50">
                                        {columns.map(col => <td key={col.key} className={`px-4 py-3 align-top ${col.align === "right" ? "text-right" : ""}`}>{col.render ? col.render(row) : row[col.key]}</td>)}
                                    </tr>
                                ))}
                                {!rows.length && <tr><td colSpan={columns.length} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">No data available for the current selection.</td></tr>}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </section>
            );
        }

        function App() {
            const [returnFile, setReturnFile] = useState(null);
            const [shopifyFile, setShopifyFile] = useState(null);
            const [returnRows, setReturnRows] = useState([]);
            const [shopifyRows, setShopifyRows] = useState([]);
            const [loading, setLoading] = useState("");
            const [error, setError] = useState("");
            const [activeStore, setActiveStore] = useState("all");
            const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
            const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState("dateKey");
            const [sortDesc, setSortDesc] = useState(true);

            async function handleReturnUpload(event) {
                const file = event.target.files?.[0];
                if (!file) return;
                setError("");
                setLoading("Reading ReturnGO export...");
                try {
                    const rows = await readTabularFile(file);
                    const normalized = rows.map(normalizeReturnRow).filter(Boolean);
                    setReturnFile(file);
                    setReturnRows(normalized);
                    setActiveStore("all");
                } catch (err) {
                    setError(`ReturnGO upload failed: ${err.message}`);
                } finally {
                    setLoading("");
                    event.target.value = "";
                }
            }

            async function handleShopifyUpload(event) {
                const file = event.target.files?.[0];
                if (!file) return;
                setError("");
                setLoading("Reading Shopify orders...");
                try {
                    const rows = await readTabularFile(file);
                    setShopifyFile(file);
                    setShopifyRows(normalizeShopifyRows(rows));
                } catch (err) {
                    setError(`Shopify upload failed: ${err.message}`);
                } finally {
                    setLoading("");
                    event.target.value = "";
                }
            }

            const analytics = useMemo(() => buildAnalytics(returnRows, shopifyRows, activeStore, search), [returnRows, shopifyRows, activeStore, search]);

            const detailedRows = useMemo(() => {
                const sorted = [...analytics.scopedReturns].sort((a, b) => {
                    const av = a[sortKey];
                    const bv = b[sortKey];
                    if (av < bv) return sortDesc ? 1 : -1;
                    if (av > bv) return sortDesc ? -1 : 1;
                    return 0;
                });
                return sorted;
            }, [analytics.scopedReturns, sortKey, sortDesc]);

            function exportCsv() {
                const headers = ["Date", "Store", "RMA", "Order", "SKU", "Barcode", "Product ID", "Variant ID", "Quantity", "Value", "Reason", "Answer", "Resolution", "Resolution Group", "Status"];
                const rows = detailedRows.map(row => [
                    row.dateKey, row.storeName, row.rmaId, row.orderId, row.sku, row.barcode, row.productId, row.variantId, row.qty, row.value, row.reason, row.answer, row.resolution, row.resolutionGroup, row.status
                ]);
                const csv = [headers, ...rows].map(row => row.map(cell => `"${clean(cell).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(",")).join("\n");
                const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                const link = document.createElement("a");
                link.href = url;
                link.download = `Bounty_Returns_Executive_Export_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
                document.body.appendChild(link);
                link.click();
                document.body.removeChild(link);
                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            }

            const monthlyChart = {
                type: "bar",
                data: {
                    labels: analytics.monthRows.map(row => row.label),
                    datasets: [
                        { label: "Returned orders", data: analytics.monthRows.map(row => row.returnedOrders), backgroundColor: ECOMPLETE, yAxisID: "y" },
                        { label: "Shopify orders", data: analytics.monthRows.map(row => row.orders), backgroundColor: "#94a3b8", yAxisID: "y" },
                        { label: "Return rate %", data: analytics.monthRows.map(row => row.rate), borderColor: "#f59e0b", backgroundColor: "#f59e0b", type: "line", yAxisID: "rate", tension: 0.35 }
                    ]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, interaction: { mode: "index", intersect: false }, plugins: { legend: { position: "bottom" } }, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true }, rate: { beginAtZero: true, position: "right", grid: { drawOnChartArea: false }, ticks: { callback: value => `${value}%` } } } }
            };
            const brandRateChart = {
                type: "bar",
                data: {
                    labels: analytics.brandRows.map(row => row.name),
                    datasets: [{ label: "Return rate %", data: analytics.brandRows.map(row => row.returnRate), backgroundColor: analytics.brandRows.map(row => row.color) }]
                },
                options: { indexAxis: "y", responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctx => `Return rate: ${pct(ctx.raw || 0)}` } } }, scales: { x: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { callback: value => `${value}%` } } } }
            };
            const reasonChart = {
                type: "doughnut",
                data: {
                    labels: analytics.reasonRows.slice(0, 8).map(row => row.reason),
                    datasets: [{ data: analytics.reasonRows.slice(0, 8).map(row => row.items), backgroundColor: ["#002244", "#2563eb", "#0891b2", "#16a34a", "#f59e0b", "#dc2626", "#7c3aed", "#64748b"] }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: { position: "bottom" }, tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctx => `${ctx.label}: ${number(ctx.raw)} items` } } } }
            };
            const resolutionChart = {
                type: "pie",
                data: {
                    labels: analytics.resolutionRows.map(row => row.resolution),
                    datasets: [{ data: analytics.resolutionRows.map(row => row.items), backgroundColor: ["#002244", "#0f766e", "#f59e0b", "#7c3aed", "#dc2626", "#64748b"] }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: { position: "bottom" } } }
            };
            const productChart = {
                type: "bar",
                data: {
                    labels: analytics.productRows.slice(0, 12).map(row => row.productKey),
                    datasets: [{ label: "Returned items", data: analytics.productRows.slice(0, 12).map(row => row.items), backgroundColor: "#0f766e" }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, scales: { x: { ticks: { maxRotation: 55, minRotation: 35 } }, y: { beginAtZero: true } } }
            };

            const brandColumns = [
                { key: "name", label: "Brand" },
                { key: "orderVolume", label: "Shopify Orders", align: "right", render: row => number(row.orderVolume) },
                { key: "returnedOrders", label: "Returned Orders", align: "right", render: row => number(row.returnedOrders) },
                { key: "returnRate", label: "Return Rate", align: "right", render: row => row.returnRate === null ? "n/a" : pct(row.returnRate) },
                { key: "items", label: "Items", align: "right", render: row => number(row.items) },
                { key: "value", label: "Return Value", align: "right", render: row => currency(row.value) },
                { key: "topReason", label: "Top Reason" },
                { key: "topResolution", label: "Top Resolution" }
            ];
            const reasonColumns = [
                { key: "reason", label: "Return Reason" },
                { key: "items", label: "Items", align: "right", render: row => number(row.items) },
                { key: "share", label: "Share", align: "right", render: row => pct(row.share) },
                { key: "value", label: "Value", align: "right", render: row => currency(row.value) },
                { key: "topAnswer", label: "Most Common Detail" }
            ];
            const resolutionColumns = [
                { key: "resolution", label: "Resolution" },
                { key: "items", label: "Items", align: "right", render: row => number(row.items) },
                { key: "rmas", label: "RMAs", align: "right", render: row => number(row.rmas) },
                { key: "share", label: "Share", align: "right", render: row => pct(row.share) },
                { key: "value", label: "Value", align: "right", render: row => currency(row.value) }
            ];
            const productColumns = [
                { key: "productKey", label: "SKU/Product ID" },
                { key: "barcode", label: "Barcode" },
                { key: "variantId", label: "Variant ID" },
                { key: "items", label: "Items", align: "right", render: row => number(row.items) },
                { key: "value", label: "Value", align: "right", render: row => currency(row.value) },
                { key: "topReason", label: "Dominant Reason" },
                { key: "topResolution", label: "Top Resolution" }
            ];
            const detailColumns = [
                { key: "dateKey", label: "Date", render: row => <button className="font-semibold text-slate-700" onClick={() => { setSortKey("dateKey"); setSortDesc(sortKey === "dateKey" ? !sortDesc : true); }}>{row.dateKey}</button> },
                { key: "storeName", label: "Store" },
                { key: "rmaId", label: "RMA" },
                { key: "orderId", label: "Order" },
                { key: "sku", label: "SKU/Product", render: row => row.productKey },
                { key: "qty", label: "Qty", align: "right", render: row => number(row.qty) },
                { key: "value", label: "Value", align: "right", render: row => currency(row.value, 2) },
                { key: "reason", label: "Reason" },
                { key: "answer", label: "Detail" },
                { key: "resolutionGroup", label: "Resolution" },
                { key: "status", label: "Status" }
            ];

            return (
                <div className="min-h-screen">
                    <header className="bg-white border-b border-slate-200">
                        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-5 py-7">
                            <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between gap-5">
                                <div>
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
                                        <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg text-white flex items-center justify-center" style={{ backgroundColor: ECOMPLETE }}>
                                            <i className="fa-solid fa-chart-line"></i>
                                        </div>
                                        <div>
                                            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight">Bounty Brands Returns Executive Dashboard</h1>
                                            <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">ReturnGO and Shopify comparison for 1 Jan 2026 to 1 May 2026</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                    <button onClick={exportCsv} disabled={!returnRows.length} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-white text-sm font-bold disabled:opacity-40">
                                        <i className="fa-solid fa-file-csv mr-2"></i>Export filtered CSV
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </header>

                    <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-5 py-6 space-y-6">
                        <section className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                            <UploadCard title="Upload merged ReturnGO export" required file={returnFile} onChange={handleReturnUpload} icon="fa-rotate-left" note="Accepts .xlsx, .xls or .csv with ReturnGO RMA rows" />
                            <UploadCard title="Upload merged Shopify orders" file={shopifyFile} onChange={handleShopifyUpload} icon="fa-bag-shopping" note="Optional, unlocks order-volume and return-rate comparison" />
                        </section>

                        {(loading || error) && (
                            <section className={`rounded-lg p-4 text-sm font-semibold ${error ? "bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200" : "bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200"}`}>
                                {loading || error}
                            </section>
                        )}

                        <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-4 shadow-sm flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-4 lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                <button onClick={() => setActiveStore("all")} className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold ${activeStore === "all" ? "bg-slate-900 text-white" : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700"}`}>All Brands</button>
                                {STORES.map(store => (
                                    <button key={store.id} onClick={() => setActiveStore(store.id)} className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold ${activeStore === store.id ? "text-white" : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700"}`} style={activeStore === store.id ? { backgroundColor: store.color } : {}}>
                                        {store.name}
                                    </button>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                            <div className="relative w-full lg:w-80">
                                <i className="fa-solid fa-search absolute left-3 top-3 text-slate-400"></i>
                                <input value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-sm" placeholder="Search RMA, order, SKU, reason..." />
                            </div>
                        </section>

                        {!returnRows.length ? (
                            <section className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-200 shadow-sm p-10 text-center">
                                <i className="fa-solid fa-file-excel text-5xl text-slate-300 mb-4"></i>
                                <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">Upload the ReturnGO export to begin</h2>
                                <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2 max-w-2xl mx-auto">The dashboard will normalize the file in your browser, classify each order by brand prefix, and then reveal executive KPIs, charts, drill-down tables and commentary.</p>
                            </section>
                        ) : (
                            <>
                                <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                                    <KpiCard label="Return RMAs" value={number(analytics.kpis.rmas)} sub={`${number(analytics.kpis.returnedOrders)} unique returned orders`} icon="fa-file-signature" tone="slate" />
                                    <KpiCard label="Returned Items" value={number(analytics.kpis.items)} sub={`${currency(analytics.kpis.value)} estimated return value`} icon="fa-box-open" tone="blue" />
                                    <KpiCard label="Shopify Orders" value={shopifyRows.length ? number(analytics.kpis.shopifyOrderCount) : "n/a"} sub={shopifyRows.length ? `${currency(analytics.kpis.shopifyValue)} order value` : "Upload Shopify orders for denominator"} icon="fa-cart-shopping" tone="white" />
                                    <KpiCard label="Order Return Rate" value={analytics.kpis.returnRate === null ? "n/a" : pct(analytics.kpis.returnRate)} sub={analytics.kpis.valueRate === null ? "Value rate unavailable" : `${pct(analytics.kpis.valueRate)} return value vs order value`} icon="fa-percent" tone="green" />
                                </section>

                                <section className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                                    <div className="xl:col-span-2">
                                        <ChartPanel title="Monthly Returned Orders vs Shopify Order Volume" subtitle="Hover for monthly returned orders, order volume and return rate." config={monthlyChart} />
                                    </div>
                                    <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm p-5">
                                        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 mb-4">Expert Commentary</h3>
                                        <div className="space-y-3">
                                            {analytics.commentary.map((line, idx) => (
                                                <div key={idx} className="flex gap-3 text-sm">
                                                    <span className="mt-1 w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-black text-slate-600">{idx + 1}</span>
                                                    <p className="text-slate-700 leading-6">{line}</p>
                                                </div>
                                            ))}
                                        </div>
                                    </section>
                                </section>

                                <section className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                    <ChartPanel title="Return Rate by Brand" subtitle="Uses unique Shopify orders as denominator when uploaded." config={brandRateChart} compact />
                                    <ChartPanel title="Return Reason Distribution" subtitle="Top reasons by returned item quantity." config={reasonChart} compact />
                                    <ChartPanel title="Resolution Preference Mix" subtitle="Refund, gift card, store credit, exchange and cancellation selections." config={resolutionChart} compact />
                                    <ChartPanel title="Top SKU/Product ID Concentration" subtitle="Product view uses SKU first, then product ID, variant ID or barcode." config={productChart} compact />
                                </section>

                                <DataTable title="Brand Performance Drill-Down" rows={analytics.brandRows} columns={brandColumns} maxHeight="max-h-[360px]" />

                                <section className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                    <DataTable title="Return Reason Drill-Down" rows={analytics.reasonRows} columns={reasonColumns} />
                                    <DataTable title="Resolution Preference Drill-Down" rows={analytics.resolutionRows} columns={resolutionColumns} />
                                </section>

                                <section className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                                    <div className="xl:col-span-2">
                                        <DataTable title="SKU/Product ID Drill-Down" rows={analytics.productRows.slice(0, 100)} columns={productColumns} />
                                    </div>
                                    <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm p-5">
                                        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 mb-4">Status and Process View</h3>
                                        <div className="space-y-3 mb-6">
                                            {analytics.statusRows.map(row => (
                                                <div key={row.status}>
                                                    <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                                                        <span className="font-semibold">{row.status}</span>
                                                        <span>{number(row.items)} items | {pct(row.share)}</span>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <div className="h-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                                                        <div className="h-full bg-slate-800" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, row.share)}%` }}></div>
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                            ))}
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 text-sm">
                                            <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-4">
                                                <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 font-bold">Fulfilment to return request</p>
                                                <p className="text-2xl font-black mt-1">{analytics.lifecycle.avgFulfillmentToReturnDays === null ? "n/a" : `${analytics.lifecycle.avgFulfillmentToReturnDays.toFixed(1)} days`}</p>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-4">
                                                <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 font-bold">Request to refund/credit</p>
                                                <p className="text-2xl font-black mt-1">{analytics.lifecycle.avgRefundDays === null ? "n/a" : `${analytics.lifecycle.avgRefundDays.toFixed(1)} days`}</p>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-4">
                                                <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 font-bold">Shipped back to delivered</p>
                                                <p className="text-2xl font-black mt-1">{analytics.lifecycle.avgReceivedDays === null ? "n/a" : `${analytics.lifecycle.avgReceivedDays.toFixed(1)} days`}</p>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </section>
                                </section>

                                <section className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
                                    <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between gap-3">
                                        <div>
                                            <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900">Detailed Normalized Return Rows</h3>
                                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Click the date column to toggle date sorting. Use the search box above for quick filtering.</p>
                                        </div>
                                        <button onClick={exportCsv} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-white text-sm font-bold">
                                            <i className="fa-solid fa-download mr-2"></i>Export current view
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                    <DataTable title="" rows={detailedRows} columns={detailColumns} maxHeight="max-h-[620px]" hideHeader />
                                </section>
                            </>
                        )}
                    </main>
                </div>
            );
        }

        ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(<App />);
    </script>
</body>
</html>

import os
import toml
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text, exc

def get_db_url_and_connect_args():
    """Reads secrets and constructs the database URL and connection arguments."""
    try:
        # Construct the full path to the secrets.toml file
        script_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
        secrets_path = os.path.join(script_dir, ".streamlit", "secrets.toml")

        if not os.path.exists(secrets_path):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Secrets file not found at: {secrets_path}")

        secrets = toml.load(secrets_path)
        conn = secrets["connections"]["postgresql"]
        
        dialect = conn.get("dialect", "postgresql")
        driver = conn.get("driver")
        user = conn["username"]
        password = conn["password"]
        host = conn["host"]
        port = conn["port"]
        database = conn["database"]
        
        # Construct the database URL, including the driver if specified
        db_url = f"{dialect}{f'+{driver}' if driver else ''}://{user}:{password}@{host}:{port}/{database}"

        connect_args = {}
        # Handle SSL settings based on the specified driver
        if driver == "pg8000" and conn.get("sslmode") == "require":
            # pg8000 uses 'ssl_context' in connect_args
            connect_args["ssl_context"] = True
        elif "sslmode" in conn:
            # Other drivers like psycopg2 expect 'sslmode' as a URL parameter
            db_url += f"?sslmode={conn['sslmode']}"

        return db_url, connect_args
    except (FileNotFoundError, KeyError) as e:
        print(f"Error reading database configuration: {e}")
        return None, None

def main():
    """Connects to the PostgreSQL database and reports table counts."""
    db_url, connect_args = get_db_url_and_connect_args()

    if not db_url:
        return

    print(f"Connecting to PostgreSQL database at: {db_url.split('@')[-1]}")

    try:
        engine = create_engine(db_url, connect_args=connect_args)
        
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            print("✅ Connection successful!")
            
            # Check for rmas table
            try:
                rma_count_result = connection.execute(text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM rmas")).scalar_one_or_none()
                print(f"   - Found 'rmas' table with {rma_count_result} rows.")
            except exc.ProgrammingError:
                print("   - 'rmas' table not found.")

            # Check for sync_logs table
            try:
                sync_logs_count_result = connection.execute(text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_logs")).scalar_one_or_none()
                print(f"   - Found 'sync_logs' table with {sync_logs_count_result} rows.")
            except exc.ProgrammingError:
                print("   - 'sync_logs' table not found.")

    except exc.OperationalError as e:
        print(f"❌ Connection failed: Could not connect to the database.")
        print(f"   Error: {e}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ An unexpected error occurred: {e}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

--- Returngo/get_inbound_details.py ---

import os
import sys
import requests
import base64
import json
from datetime import datetime

def get_inbound_details(inbound_id):
    """
    Retrieves details for a specific inbound from the ParcelNinja API.
    """
    # --- Configuration ---
    username = os.environ.get("PNJ_Levis_username")
    password = os.environ.get("PNJ_Levis_password")

    if not all([username, password]):
        print("Error: Missing required environment variables (PNJ_Levis_username, PNJ_Levis_password)")
        return

    # --- Authentication ---
    auth_str = f"{username}:{password}"
    encoded_auth_str = base64.b64encode(auth_str.encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')

    headers = {
        "Accept": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Basic {encoded_auth_str}"
    }

    url = f"https://storeapi.parcelninja.com/api/v1/inbounds/{inbound_id}"

    # --- API Request ---
    try:
        print(f"Requesting details for Inbound ID: {inbound_id}...")
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
        response.raise_for_status()

        print("Request successful!\n")
        inbound_data = response.json()

        # --- Print Key Details ---
        client_id = inbound_data.get('clientId', 'N/A')
        customer = inbound_data.get('deliveryInfo', {}).get('customer', 'N/A')
        create_date_str = inbound_data.get('createDate', '')
        status = inbound_data.get('status', {}).get('description', 'N/A')

        try:
            create_date = datetime.strptime(create_date_str, '%Y%m%d%H%M%S').strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            create_date = create_date_str

        print("--- Inbound Summary ---")
        print(f"  Parcelninja ID: {inbound_data.get('id', 'N/A')}")
        print(f"  Client Reference: {client_id}")
        print(f"  Customer:         {customer}")
        print(f"  Status:           {status}")
        print(f"  Created:          {create_date}")
        print("\n--- Items in Inbound ---")

        items = inbound_data.get('items', [])
        if items:
            print(f"{'SKU (itemNo)':<25} {'Name':<50} {'Qty':<5} {'Return Reason'}")
            print("-" * 100)
            for item in items:
                print(f"{item.get('itemNo', 'N/A'):<25} {item.get('name', 'N/A'):<50} {item.get('qty', 'N/A'):<5} {item.get('returnReason', '-')}")
        else:
            print("No items found in this inbound.")

    except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as http_err:
        print(f"HTTP error occurred: {http_err}")
        if http_err.response.status_code == 404:
            print(f"Inbound with ID '{inbound_id}' not found.")
        else:
            print(f"Response body: {http_err.response.text}")
    except Exception as err:
        print(f"An error occurred: {err}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("Usage: python get_inbound_details.py <inbound_id>")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    get_inbound_details(sys.argv[1])

--- Returngo/get_parcelninja_inbounds.py ---


import os
import requests
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

def get_inbounds():
    """
    Retrieves inbounds from the ParcelNinja API for the last 3 days.
    """
    username = os.environ.get("PNJ_Levis_username")
    password = os.environ.get("PNJ_Levis_password")
    store_id = os.environ.get("PNJ_Levis_StoreID")

    if not all([username, password, store_id]):
        print("Error: Missing one or more required environment variables (PNJ_Levis_username, PNJ_Levis_password, PNJ_Levis_StoreID)")
        return

    auth_str = f"{username}:{password}"
    encoded_auth_str = base64.b64encode(auth_str.encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')

    headers = {
        "Accept": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Basic {encoded_auth_str}"
    }

    end_date = datetime.now()
    start_date = end_date - timedelta(days=3)

    params = {
        "startDate": start_date.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
        "endDate": end_date.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
        "pageSize": 100,
        "page": 1
    }

    url = "https://storeapi.parcelninja.com/api/v1/inbounds/"

    try:
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers, params=params)
        response.raise_for_status()  # Raise an exception for bad status codes

        print("Request successful!")
        # Writing response to a file
        with open("parcelninja_inbounds.json", "w") as f:
            f.write(response.text)
        print("Response saved to parcelninja_inbounds.json")


    except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as http_err:
        print(f"HTTP error occurred: {http_err}")
        print(f"Response body: {response.text}")
    except Exception as err:
        print(f"An error occurred: {err}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    get_inbounds()


--- Returngo/get_recent_inbounds.py ---

import os
import requests
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import json

def get_recent_inbounds():
    """
    Retrieves the 10 most recently created inbounds from the ParcelNinja API
    from the last 5 days.

    Note: The Parcelninja API for inbounds does not support sorting by "last updated".
    This script sorts by "Date Created" in descending order to get the most
    recently created inbounds.
    """
    # --- Configuration ---
    # It's best practice to load credentials from environment variables
    # or a secure secret management system, not hardcode them.
    username = os.environ.get("PNJ_Levis_username")
    password = os.environ.get("PNJ_Levis_password")

    if not all([username, password]):
        print("Error: Missing required environment variables (PNJ_Levis_username, PNJ_Levis_password)")
        return

    # --- Authentication ---
    auth_str = f"{username}:{password}"
    encoded_auth_str = base64.b64encode(auth_str.encode('utf-8')).decode('utf-8')

    headers = {
        "Accept": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Basic {encoded_auth_str}"
    }

    # --- Parameters ---
    end_date = datetime.now()
    start_date = end_date - timedelta(days=5)

    # Based on the parcelninja.txt documentation for the /inbounds/ endpoint:
    # - col: Sort field
    #     0 = Reference
    #     1 = Supplier
    #     2 = Estimated Arrival Date
    #     3 = Total Items
    #     4 = Date Created (used by this script)
    # - colOrder='desc' sorts in descending order (most recent first)
    params = {
        "startDate": start_date.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
        "endDate": end_date.strftime("%Y%m%d"),
        "pageSize": 10,
        "page": 1,
        "col": 4, # Sort by Date Created
        "colOrder": "desc" # Most recent first
    }

    url = "https://storeapi.parcelninja.com/api/v1/inbounds/"

    # --- API Request ---
    try:
        print(f"Requesting inbounds from {params['startDate']} to {params['endDate']}...")
        response = requests.get(url, headers=headers, params=params)
        response.raise_for_status()  # Raise an exception for bad status codes (4xx or 5xx)

        print("Request successful! Here are the 10 most recently created inbounds:\n")
        inbounds_data = response.json()

        if "inbounds" in inbounds_data and inbounds_data["inbounds"]:
            print(f"{'ID':<10} {'Client ID':<20} {'Type':<25} {'Supplier/Customer':<20} {'Est. Arrival':<15} {'Status':<30} {'Items'}")
            print("-" * 135)
            for inbound in inbounds_data["inbounds"]:
                inbound_id = inbound.get('id', 'N/A')
                client_id = inbound.get('clientId', 'N/A')
                create_date_str = inbound.get('createDate', '')
                inbound_type = inbound.get('type', {}).get('description', 'N/A')
                supplier = inbound.get('deliveryInfo', {}).get('customer', 'N/A')
                est_arrival_str = inbound.get('deliveryInfo', {}).get('estimatedArrivalDate', '')
                status_desc = inbound.get('status', {}).get('description', 'N/A')
                num_items = inbound.get('numItems', 'N/A')

                # Format date for readability
                try:
                    create_date = datetime.strptime(create_date_str, '%Y%m%d%H%M%S').strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    create_date = create_date_str
                
                # Format est arrival date for readability
                try:
                    est_arrival = datetime.strptime(est_arrival_str, '%Y%m%d%H%M%S').strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    est_arrival = 'N/A'

                print(
                    f"{inbound_id:<10} {client_id:<20} {inbound_type:<25} "
                    f"{supplier:<20} {est_arrival:<15} {status_desc:<30} {num_items}"
                )
        else:
            print("No inbounds found in the specified date range.")

    except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as http_err:
        print(f"HTTP error occurred: {http_err}")
        print(f"Response body: {response.text}")
    except Exception as err:
        print(f"An error occurred: {err}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    get_recent_inbounds()

--- Returngo/import_data.py ---

import pandas as pd
import toml
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text

def get_db_url():
    secrets = toml.load(r"c:\Users\Taswell\OneDrive\Documents\GitHub\Returngo\.streamlit\secrets.toml")
    conn = secrets["connections"]["postgresql"]
    return f"{conn['dialect']}+{conn['driver']}://{conn['username']}:{conn['password']}@{conn['host']}:{conn['port']}/{conn['database']}?sslmode={conn['sslmode']}"

def create_table(engine):
    create_table_query = text("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rmas (
        rma_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        store_url TEXT,
        status TEXT,
        created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
        json_data JSONB,
        last_fetched TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
        courier_status TEXT,
        courier_last_checked TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
        received_first_seen TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
    );
    """)
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(create_table_query)
            connection.commit()
        print("Table 'rmas' is ready.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error creating table: {e}")
        raise

def import_csv_to_db(engine, filepath):
    try:
        df = pd.read_csv(filepath)
        
        for col in ['created_at', 'last_fetched', 'courier_last_checked', 'received_first_seen']:
            df[col] = pd.to_datetime(df[col], errors='coerce')
        
        print(f"Loaded {len(df)} records from CSV.")
        
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            df.to_sql('temp_rmas', connection, if_exists='replace', index=False)
            
            insert_query = text("""
            INSERT INTO rmas (rma_id, store_url, status, created_at, json_data, last_fetched, courier_status, courier_last_checked, received_first_seen)
            SELECT rma_id, store_url, status, created_at, CAST(json_data AS JSONB), last_fetched, courier_status, courier_last_checked, received_first_seen
            FROM temp_rmas
            ON CONFLICT (rma_id) DO UPDATE SET
                store_url = EXCLUDED.store_url,
                status = EXCLUDED.status,
                created_at = EXCLUDED.created_at,
                json_data = EXCLUDED.json_data,
                last_fetched = EXCLUDED.last_fetched,
                courier_status = EXCLUDED.courier_status,
                courier_last_checked = EXCLUDED.courier_last_checked,
                received_first_seen = EXCLUDED.received_first_seen;
            """)
            connection.execute(insert_query)
            connection.commit()
            
        print("Successfully imported data into 'rmas' table.")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error importing data: {e}")
        raise

def main():
    print("Importing Levi's Web DB to PostgreSQL")

    try:
        db_url = get_db_url()
        engine = create_engine(db_url)
        print("Successfully connected to the PostgreSQL database.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Failed to connect to PostgreSQL: {e}")
        return

    create_table(engine)

    csv_path = "download_levis-web_db.csv"
    import_csv_to_db(engine, csv_path)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def load_user_settings() -> UserSettings:
    """Load user settings from file or create default."""
    if USER_SETTINGS_FILE.exists():
        try:
            with open(USER_SETTINGS_FILE, 'rb') as f:
                loaded = pickle.load(f)
                if isinstance(loaded, UserSettings):
                    return loaded
                logger.warning("Ignoring invalid user settings payload type: %s", type(loaded).__name__)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Unable to load user settings from %s: %s", USER_SETTINGS_FILE, e)
    return UserSettings()

def save_user_settings(settings: UserSettings):
    """Save user settings to file."""
    try:
        with open(USER_SETTINGS_FILE, 'wb') as f:
            pickle.dump(settings, f)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to save user settings: {e}")

def load_performance_metrics() -> PerformanceMetrics:
    """Load performance metrics from file."""
    if PERFORMANCE_FILE.exists():
        try:
            with open(PERFORMANCE_FILE, 'rb') as f:
                loaded = pickle.load(f)
                if isinstance(loaded, PerformanceMetrics):
                    return loaded
                logger.warning("Ignoring invalid performance metrics payload type: %s", type(loaded).__name__)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Unable to load performance metrics from %s: %s", PERFORMANCE_FILE, e)
    return PerformanceMetrics()

def save_performance_metrics(metrics: PerformanceMetrics):
    """Save performance metrics to file."""
    try:
        with open(PERFORMANCE_FILE, 'wb') as f:
            pickle.dump(metrics, f)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Failed to save performance metrics: {e}")


def persist_performance_metrics(metrics: PerformanceMetrics):
    """Persist metrics to disk and update session state when available."""
    save_performance_metrics(metrics)
    if "performance_metrics" in st.session_state:
        st.session_state.performance_metrics = metrics


def update_performance_metrics(api_time: float):
    """Update performance metrics with new API call time."""
    metrics = load_performance_metrics()
    metrics.api_call_times.append(api_time)
    if len(metrics.api_call_times) > 100:
        metrics.api_call_times = metrics.api_call_times[-100:]
    if metrics.api_call_times:
        metrics.avg_response_time = sum(metrics.api_call_times) / len(metrics.api_call_times)
    save_performance_metrics(metrics)
    if "performance_metrics" in st.session_state:
        st.session_state.performance_metrics = metrics

# ==========================================
# 2. SAFE UTILITIES
# ==========================================
def safe_parse_date_iso(s: Optional[str]) -> Optional[datetime]:
    """Parses ISO8601 timestamp or returns None."""
    if not s or not isinstance(s, str):
        return None
    try:
        return datetime.fromisoformat(s.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    except Exception:
        return None


def format_date(dt: Optional[datetime], fmt: str = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M") -> str:
    """Safely format a datetime, returning '-' if None."""
    if not dt:
        return "-"
    return dt.strftime(fmt)


def format_recent_sync_detail() -> str:
    """Builds a user-facing summary of recent sync/update activity."""
    recent_fetch = st.session_state.get("last_sync_time")
    recent_rma_update = st.session_state.get("last_rma_update_time")
    session_access_time = st.session_state.get("session_access_time")

    if recent_fetch is None:
        recent_fetch = session_access_time
    if recent_rma_update is None:
        recent_rma_update = session_access_time

    fetch_str = format_date(recent_fetch, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S") if recent_fetch else "Never"
    rma_update_str = format_date(recent_rma_update, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S") if recent_rma_update else "Never"
    return f"Recent Sync: Fetch {fetch_str} | RMA Update {rma_update_str}"


def safe_get(data: Any, path: str, default=None) -> Any:
    """Safely extract nested dict/list value using dot notation (e.g. 'shipments.0.id')."""
    if data is None:
        return default
    keys = path.split(".")
    val = data
    for k in keys:
        if isinstance(val, dict):
            val = val.get(k, default)
        elif isinstance(val, (list, tuple)):
            try:
                idx = int(k)
                if 0 <= idx < len(val):
                    val = val[idx]
                else:
                    return default
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                return default
        else:
            return default
        
        if val is None:
            return default
    return val


def compute_days_since(from_date: Optional[datetime]) -> int:
    """Calculate how many days ago `from_date` was."""
    if not from_date:
        return 0
    now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    from_date_utc = from_date.astimezone(timezone.utc) if from_date.tzinfo else from_date.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    delta = now_utc - from_date_utc
    return delta.days


def get_event_date(rma_data: dict, event_name: str) -> Optional[datetime]:
    """Extracts the date for a specific event from the RMA events list."""
    # Check both top-level events and rmaSummary.events
    events = rma_data.get("events", [])
    if not events:
        events = safe_get(rma_data, "rmaSummary.events", [])
    
    for event in events:
        if event.get("eventName") == event_name:
            return safe_parse_date_iso(event.get("eventDate"))
    return None


def get_received_date_from_events_or_comments(rma_data: dict) -> Optional[datetime]:
    """
    Gets the shipment received date, prioritizing the 'SHIPMENT_RECEIVED' event,
    but falling back to the earliest system comment containing 'RECEIVED'.
    """
    # 1. Prioritize the official event
    event_date = get_event_date(rma_data, "SHIPMENT_RECEIVED")
    if event_date:
        return event_date

    # 2. Fallback to searching comments
    comments = safe_get(rma_data, "comments", [])
    if not comments:
        return None

    received_comment_dates = []
    for comment in comments:
        html_text = comment.get("htmlText", "")
        # The user's snippet is: "Shipment &lt;strong&gt;RECEIVED&lt;/strong&gt;"
        if "RECEIVED" in html_text.upper():
            comment_date = safe_parse_date_iso(comment.get("datetime"))
            if comment_date:
                received_comment_dates.append(comment_date)
    
    # Return the earliest date found in comments if any
    return min(received_comment_dates) if received_comment_dates else None

def get_done_date_from_comments(rma_data: dict) -> Optional[datetime]:
    """
    Finds the timestamp when the RMA status was updated to 'Done' from comments.
    """
    comments = safe_get(rma_data, "comments", [])
    if not comments:
        return None

    done_comment_dates = []
    # The pattern is usually "status was updated to <strong>Done</strong>"
    # A case-insensitive search for "status was updated to" and "done" is robust.
    for comment in comments:
        html_text = comment.get("htmlText", "").lower()
        if "status was updated to" in html_text and "done" in html_text:
            comment_date = safe_parse_date_iso(comment.get("datetime"))
            if comment_date:
                done_comment_dates.append(comment_date)

    # Return the most recent 'Done' comment date
    return max(done_comment_dates) if done_comment_dates else None


def get_resolution_types(rma_data: dict) -> Set[str]:
    """Gets a set of all unique resolution types from the RMA items."""
    items = safe_get(rma_data, "items", [])
    if not items:
        return set()
    return {item.get("resolutionType") for item in items if item.get("resolutionType")}

def has_tracking_update_comment(rma_data: dict) -> bool:
    """Checks if a comment indicating a tracking update exists."""
    comments = safe_get(rma_data, "comments", [])
    return any("was updated" in str(c.get("htmlText", "")).lower() for c in comments)

def get_requests_session() -> requests.Session:
    """Creates a session with retry logic for HTTP requests."""
    session = requests.Session()
    retry_strategy = Retry(
        total=3,
        status_forcelist=[429, 500, 502, 503, 504],
        allowed_methods=["GET", "POST"],
        backoff_factor=2,
    )
    adapter = HTTPAdapter(max_retries=retry_strategy)
    session.mount("https://", adapter)
    session.mount("http://", adapter)
    return session


def _build_parcelninja_auth_header() -> str:
    """Build ParcelNinja Basic auth header from a single shared credential source."""
    auth_str = f"{PARCELNINJA_USERNAME}:{PARCELNINJA_PASSWORD}"
    encoded_auth_str = base64.b64encode(auth_str.encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")
    return f"Basic {encoded_auth_str}"


def create_parcelninja_api_session() -> requests.Session:
    """Create a dedicated ParcelNinja API session with retry/backoff for transient failures."""
    session = requests.Session()
    retry_strategy = Retry(
        total=4,
        connect=4,
        read=4,
        status=4,
        status_forcelist=[429, 500, 502, 503, 504],
        allowed_methods=["GET"],
        backoff_factor=0.6,
        respect_retry_after_header=True,
    )
    adapter = HTTPAdapter(max_retries=retry_strategy, pool_connections=PN_MAX_EVENT_WORKERS, pool_maxsize=PN_MAX_EVENT_WORKERS * 2)
    session.mount("https://", adapter)
    session.mount("http://", adapter)
    return session

def get_parcel_session() -> requests.Session:
    """Creates a dedicated session for the Parcel Ninja scraper with retries."""
    session = getattr(threading.local(), "parcel_session", None)
    if session is not None:
        return session

    session = requests.Session()
    retry_strategy = Retry(
        total=3,
        status_forcelist=[429, 500, 502, 503, 504],
        allowed_methods=["GET"],
        backoff_factor=1,
        respect_retry_after_header=True,
    )
    adapter = HTTPAdapter(max_retries=retry_strategy)
    session.mount("https://", adapter)
    # Initialize _thread_local if it doesn't exist
    setattr(threading.local(), "parcel_session", session)
    return session


def update_rate_limit_info(response_headers: Mapping[str, str]):
    """Updates the global rate limit state from response headers."""
    with RATE_LIMIT_LOCK:
        if "X-RateLimit-Remaining" in response_headers:
            RATE_LIMIT_INFO["remaining"] = response_headers.get("X-RateLimit-Remaining")
        if "X-RateLimit-Limit" in response_headers:
            RATE_LIMIT_INFO["limit"] = response_headers.get("X-RateLimit-Limit")
        if "X-RateLimit-Reset" in response_headers:
            reset_str = response_headers.get("X-RateLimit-Reset")
            if reset_str:
                try:
                    RATE_LIMIT_INFO["reset"] = datetime.fromtimestamp(int(reset_str), tz=timezone.utc)
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    pass
        RATE_LIMIT_INFO["updated_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
